 53+  667A CD B8 66             CALL    read_byte_raw
 54+  667D 17                   RLA
 55+  667E 38 F8                JR      C, .wait_rtc
 56+  6680
 57+  6680                      ; Вычитываем BCD данные с атомарным сохранением в текстовые буферы
 58+  6680                      ; 1. Секунды (Ячейка #00)

 61+  6685 CD DD 66             CALL    split_bcd

 63+  668D 32 47 66       LD (txt_sec+1), A

 70+  669C 7D                   LD      A, L

 75+  66A5 CD DD 66             CALL    split_bcd
 76+  66A8 7C                   LD      A, H
 76+  66A9 32 42 66       LD (txt_hour+0), A
 77+  66AC 7D                   LD      A, L
 77+  66AD 32 43 66       LD (txt_hour+1), A
 78+  66B0
 79+  66B0 CD D6 66             CALL    close_cmos
 80+  66B3 FB                   EI
 81+  66B4 E1                   POP     HL
 81+  66B5 C1             POP BC
 81+  66B6 F1             POP AF
 82+  66B7 C9                   RET
 83+  66B8
 84+  66B8              ; --- Атомарное низкоуровневое чтение байта из К512ВИ1 ---
 85+  66B8              read_byte_raw
 86+  66B8 01 F7 DF             LD      BC, #DFF7
 87+  66BB ED 79                OUT     (C), A
 88+  66BD 01 F7 BF             LD      BC, #BFF7

 90+  66C2 C9                   RET
 91+  66C3
 92+  66C3              ; --- Атомарная низкоуровневая запись байта в К512ВИ1 ---
 93+  66C3              write_byte_raw
 94+  66C3 01 F7 DF             LD      BC, #DFF7
 95+  66C6 ED 79                OUT     (C), A
 96+  66C8 01 F7 BF             LD      BC, #BFF7
 97+  66CB ED 69                OUT     (C), L
 98+  66CD C9                   RET
 99+  66CE
100+  66CE              open_cmos

102+  66D1 3E 80                LD      A, #80
103+  66D3 ED 79                OUT     (C), A
104+  66D5 C9                   RET
105+  66D6
106+  66D6              close_cmos
107+  66D6 01 F7 EF             LD      BC, #EFF7
108+  66D9 AF                   XOR     A
109+  66DA ED 79                OUT     (C), A
110+  66DC C9                   RET
111+  66DD
112+  66DD              ; --- Распаковка BCD полубайтов в ASCII символы ---
113+  66DD              split_bcd
114+  66DD F5                   PUSH    AF
115+  66DE E6 F0                AND     %11110000
116+  66E0 0F                   RRCA
116+  66E1 0F             RRCA
116+  66E2 0F             RRCA
116+  66E3 0F             RRCA

118+  66E6 67                   LD      H, A
119+  66E7 F1                   POP     AF
120+  66E8 E6 0F                AND     %00001111
121+  66EA C6 30                ADD     A, 48
122+  66EC 6F                   LD      L, A
123+  66ED C9                   RET

 11+  66EE              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 12+  66EE              ; Load the separately stored NeoGS firmware file from TR-DOS.
 13+  66EE              ; neoprog.C layout: [WORD bootstrap_length][bootstrap bytes][firmware bytes].
 14+  66EE              ; OUT: Carry clear, complete package at #C000 and exact file length saved.
 15+  66EE              ; Uses the same verified TR-DOS 5.04T catalogue/sector API as Stage 5.
 16+  66EE              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 17+  66EE              load_neogs_firmware_file:
 17+  66EE
 18+  66EE                      ; Search the exact 8.3 catalogue name neoprog.C.
 19+  66EE 21 74 6A             ld      hl, neo_fw_filename
 20+  66F1 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
 21+  66F4 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
 22+  66F7 ED B0                ldir
 23+  66F9 3E 43                ld      a, 'C'
 24+  66FB 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
 25+  66FE 3E 09                ld      a, 9
 26+  6700 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
 27+  6703 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
 28+  6705 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 29+  6708 79                   ld      a, c
 30+  6709 FE FF                cp      #FF
 31+  670B CA C3 67             jp      z, .file_not_found
 32+  670E
 33+  670E                      ; C=#0A returns the directory slot. Fetch its full descriptor with C=#08.
 34+  670E F5                   push    af
 35+  670F AF                   xor     a
 36+  6710 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
 37+  6713 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
 38+  6716 F1                   pop     af
 39+  6717 0E 08                ld      c, 8
 40+  6719 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 41+  671C 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
 42+  671F 47                   ld      b, a
 43+  6720 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
 44+  6723 B0                   or      b
 45+  6724 C2 C7 67             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 46+  6727
 47+  6727                      ; Store the actual package length; its header separates the two code sections.
 48+  6727 2A E8 5C             ld      hl, (#5CE8)
 49+  672A 22 82 6A             ld      (neo_fw_file_length), hl
 50+  672D 7C                   ld      a, h
 51+  672E B5                   or      l
 52+  672F CA CB 67             jp      z, .bad_length
 53+  6732 7C                   ld      a, h
 54+  6733 B7                   or      a
 55+  6734 20 06                jr      nz, .package_minimum_ok
 56+  6736 7D                   ld      a, l
 57+  6737 FE 03                cp      3
 58+  6739 DA CB 67             jp      c, .bad_length
 59+  673C              .package_minimum_ok:
 60+  673C                      ; #C000 has at most 16 KiB available before address wrap at #0000.
 61+  673C 7C                   ld      a, h
 62+  673D FE 40                cp      #40
 63+  673F 38 08                jr      c, .package_size_ok
 64+  6741 C2 CB 67             jp      nz, .bad_length
 65+  6744 7D                   ld      a, l
 66+  6745 B7                   or      a
 67+  6746 C2 CB 67             jp      nz, .bad_length
 68+  6749              .package_size_ok:
 69+  6749                      ; Verify sector count = ceil(file_length / 256).
 70+  6749 2A 82 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_file_length)
 71+  674C 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
 72+  674F 19                   add     hl, de
 73+  6750 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
 74+  6753 BC                   cp      h
 75+  6754 C2 C7 67             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 76+  6757 32 7E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
 77+  675A
 78+  675A 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
 79+  675D FE 10                cp      16
 80+  675F D2 C7 67             jp      nc, .bad_descriptor
 81+  6762 32 80 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
 82+  6765 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
 83+  6768 32 7F 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
 84+  676B 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
 85+  676E 22 7C 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_buffer), hl
 86+  6771
 87+  6771              .read_sector_loop:
 88+  6771 3A 7F 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
 89+  6774 57                   ld      d, a
 90+  6775 3A 80 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
 91+  6778 5F                   ld      e, a
 92+  6779 2A 7C 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_load_buffer)
 93+  677C 06 01                ld      b, 1
 94+  677E 0E 05                ld      c, 5
 95+  6780 AF                   xor     a
 96+  6781 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
 97+  6784 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
 98+  6787 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 99+  678A 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
100+  678D 47                   ld      b, a
101+  678E 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
102+  6791 B0                   or      b
103+  6792 C2 CF 67             jp      nz, .sector_read_failed
104+  6795
105+  6795                      ; Next 256-byte sector goes to the next host buffer page.
106+  6795 2A 7C 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_load_buffer)
107+  6798 11 00 01             ld      de, 256
108+  679B 19                   add     hl, de
109+  679C 22 7C 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_buffer), hl
110+  679F
111+  679F                      ; TR-DOS internal sectors are 0..15; then advance the track.
112+  679F 3A 80 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
113+  67A2 3C                   inc     a
114+  67A3 FE 10                cp      16
115+  67A5 38 0D                jr      c, .same_track
116+  67A7 AF                   xor     a
117+  67A8 32 80 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
118+  67AB 3A 7F 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
119+  67AE 3C                   inc     a
120+  67AF 32 7F 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
121+  67B2 18 03                jr      .sector_advanced
122+  67B4              .same_track:
123+  67B4 32 80 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
124+  67B7              .sector_advanced:
125+  67B7 3A 7E 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
126+  67BA 3D                   dec     a
127+  67BB 32 7E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
128+  67BE C2 71 67             jp      nz, .read_sector_loop
129+  67C1
130+  67C1 AF                   xor     a
131+  67C2 C9                   ret
132+  67C3
133+  67C3              .file_not_found:
134+  67C3 3E 01                ld      a, 1
135+  67C5 18 0A                jr      .load_failed
136+  67C7              .bad_descriptor:
137+  67C7 3E 02                ld      a, 2
138+  67C9 18 06                jr      .load_failed
139+  67CB              .bad_length:
140+  67CB 3E 03                ld      a, 3
141+  67CD 18 02                jr      .load_failed
142+  67CF              .sector_read_failed:

144+  67D1              .load_failed:

146+  67D4 37                   scf
147+  67D5 C9                   ret
148+  67D6
149+  67D6              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
150+  67D6              ; upload_firmware
151+  67D6              ; IN : HL = neoprog package in host RAM, DE = total package length.
152+  67D6              ; OUT: Carry clear only after custom firmware sends NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK.
153+  67D6              ;
154+  67D6              ; Cold/warm reset path:
155+  67D6              ;   #F4 cold restart
156+  67D6              ;   #20 get total RAM, read three bytes to prove stock ROM is ready
157+  67D6              ;   #14 receive executable bootstrap directly into RAM at $4000
158+  67D6              ;   #13 jump to $4000
159+  67D6              ;   #A1 bootstrap ready, send length, receive #A3 length accepted
160+  67D6              ;   send custom firmware bytes to RAM $0000, receive #A2 firmware ready
161+  67D6              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
162+  67D6              upload_firmware:
162+  67D6
163+  67D6                      ; HL points to the package read once from TR-DOS; DE is its total length.
164+  67D6                      ; Layout: WORD bootstrap_length, bootstrap bytes, then firmware bytes.
165+  67D6 22 84 6A             ld      (neo_fw_package_source), hl
166+  67D9 ED 53 86 6A          ld      (neo_fw_package_length), de
167+  67DD
168+  67DD                      ; Decode and validate the bootstrap length from the package header.
169+  67DD 7A                   ld      a, d
170+  67DE B7                   or      a
171+  67DF 20 06                jr      nz, .package_header_present
172+  67E1 7B                   ld      a, e
173+  67E2 FE 03                cp      3
174+  67E4 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
175+  67E7              .package_header_present:
176+  67E7 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
177+  67E8 32 8A 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length), a
178+  67EB 23                   inc     hl
179+  67EC 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
180+  67ED 32 8B 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length+1), a
181+  67F0 2A 8A 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
182+  67F3 7C                   ld      a, h
183+  67F4 B5                   or      l
184+  67F5 CA 52 69             jp      z, .upload_failed
185+  67F8 7C                   ld      a, h
186+  67F9 FE 40                cp      #40
187+  67FB D2 52 69             jp      nc, .upload_failed
188+  67FE
189+  67FE                      ; Firmware length = package length - header - bootstrap length.
190+  67FE 2A 86 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_length)
191+  6801 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
192+  6804 B7                   or      a
193+  6805 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
194+  6807 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
195+  680A ED 5B 8A 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
196+  680E B7                   or      a
197+  680F ED 52                sbc     hl, de
198+  6811 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
199+  6814 22 8E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_length), hl
200+  6817
201+  6817                      ; Resolve both section pointers inside the same package buffer.
202+  6817 2A 84 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_source)
203+  681A 23                   inc     hl
204+  681B 23                   inc     hl
205+  681C 22 88 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_source), hl
206+  681F ED 5B 8A 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
207+  6823 19                   add     hl, de
208+  6824 22 8C 6A             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
209+  6827
210+  6827                      ; Firmware image must fit in low RAM $0000-$2FFF, below SP=$3FFF.
211+  6827 2A 8E 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
212+  682A 7C                   ld      a, h
213+  682B B7                   or      a
214+  682C C2 36 68             jp      nz, .firmware_high_nonzero
215+  682F 7D                   ld      a, l
216+  6830 B7                   or      a
217+  6831 CA 52 69             jp      z, .upload_failed
218+  6834 18 0D                jr      .firmware_length_valid
219+  6836              .firmware_high_nonzero:
220+  6836 7C                   ld      a, h
221+  6837 FE 30                cp      #30
222+  6839 38 08                jr      c, .firmware_length_valid
223+  683B C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
224+  683E 7D                   ld      a, l
225+  683F B7                   or      a
226+  6840 C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
227+  6843              .firmware_length_valid:
228+  6843 AF                   xor     a
229+  6844 32 9A 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), a
230+  6847 32 9B 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent+1), a
231+  684A 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
232+  684D DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
233+  684F 32 9C 6A             ld      (neo_fw_initial_status), a
234+  6852 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
235+  6855
236+  6855                      ; Phase 1: documented GS105a cold restart. Also returns a running
237+  6855                      ; Project 124 firmware to the stock ROM before the new bootstrap.
238+  6855 3E 01                ld      a, 1
239+  6857 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
240+  685A 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
241+  685C CD 82 69             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
242+  685F DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
243+  6862
244+  6862                      ; Phase 2: #20 returns three RAM-size bytes only after stock ROM is up.
245+  6862                      ; Retry #F4 once: if the previous run was interrupted during DMA, the
246+  6862                      ; first #F4 may have been consumed as the DMA end marker; the second
247+  6862                      ; reaches the command dispatcher after it returns to its main loop.
248+  6862 3E 02                ld      a, 2
249+  6864 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
250+  6867 CD 54 69             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
251+  686A 30 18                jr      nc, .stock_rom_ready
252+  686C 3E 01                ld      a, 1
253+  686E 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
254+  6871 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
255+  6873 CD 82 69             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
256+  6876 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
257+  6879 3E 02                ld      a, 2
258+  687B 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
259+  687E CD 54 69             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
260+  6881 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
261+  6884              .stock_rom_ready:
262+  6884
263+  6884                      ; Phase 3: use the native GS105a memory loader, not the MOD player.
264+  6884                      ; #14 RECEIVE BLOCK expects the first (negative length low) byte
265+  6884                      ; before the command, then negative length high, address low/high,
266+  6884                      ; and exactly N payload bytes. The payload is written directly to RAM.
267+  6884 3E 03                ld      a, 3
268+  6886 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
269+  6889 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
270+  688C ED 5B 8A 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
271+  6890 B7                   or      a
272+  6891 ED 52                sbc     hl, de                  ; HL = -bootstrap length
273+  6893 22 92 6A             ld      (neo_fw_block_length), hl
274+  6896 7D                   ld      a, l
275+  6897 CD C4 69             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
276+  689A DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
277+  689D 3E 14                ld      a, GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK
278+  689F CD A7 69             call    gs_boot_send_command
279+  68A2 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
280+  68A5
281+  68A5                      ; Remaining #14 header: -length high, destination $4000.
282+  68A5 3A 93 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_block_length+1)
283+  68A8 CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
284+  68AB DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
285+  68AE AF                   xor     a
286+  68AF CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination low = $00
287+  68B2 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
288+  68B5 3E 40                ld      a, #40
289+  68B7 CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination high = $40
290+  68BA DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
291+  68BD
292+  68BD                      ; Phase 4: transfer the executable bootstrap into RAM $4000.
293+  68BD 3E 04                ld      a, 4
294+  68BF 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
295+  68C2 2A 88 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_source)
296+  68C5 ED 5B 8A 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
297+  68C9              .bootstrap_block_loop:
298+  68C9 7A                   ld      a, d
299+  68CA B3                   or      e
300+  68CB 28 0B                jr      z, .bootstrap_block_done
301+  68CD 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
302+  68CE CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
303+  68D1 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
304+  68D4 23                   inc     hl
305+  68D5 1B                   dec     de
306+  68D6 18 F1                jr      .bootstrap_block_loop
307+  68D8              .bootstrap_block_done:
308+  68D8
309+  68D8                      ; Phase 5: #13 JUMP TO ADDRESS. The low byte is the command's
310+  68D8                      ; first argument and must be written before issuing the command.
311+  68D8 3E 05                ld      a, 5
312+  68DA 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
313+  68DD AF                   xor     a                       ; entry address low = $00
314+  68DE CD C4 69             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
315+  68E1 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
316+  68E4 3E 13                ld      a, GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS
317+  68E6 CD A7 69             call    gs_boot_send_command
318+  68E9 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
319+  68EC 3E 40                ld      a, #40                  ; entry address high = $40
320+  68EE CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
321+  68F1 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
322+  68F4
323+  68F4                      ; Phase 6: bootstrap has enabled RAM-over-ROM and is waiting at $0000.
324+  68F4 3E 06                ld      a, 6
325+  68F6 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
326+  68F9 CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
327+  68FC DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
328+  68FF FE A1                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
329+  6901 C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
330+  6904
331+  6904                      ; Phase 7: send firmware length low/high and require ACK #A3.
332+  6904 3E 07                ld      a, 7
333+  6906 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
334+  6909 2A 8E 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
335+  690C 7D                   ld      a, l
336+  690D CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
337+  6910 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
338+  6913 7C                   ld      a, h
339+  6914 CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
340+  6917 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
341+  691A CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
342+  691D DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
343+  6920 FE A3                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
344+  6922 C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
345+  6925
346+  6925                      ; Phase 8: stream the custom firmware to RAM address $0000.
347+  6925 3E 08                ld      a, 8
348+  6927 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
349+  692A 2A 8C 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
350+  692D ED 5B 8E 6A          ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
351+  6931              .firmware_stream_loop:
352+  6931 7A                   ld      a, d
353+  6932 B3                   or      e
354+  6933 28 0B                jr      z, .firmware_stream_done
355+  6935 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
356+  6936 CD E1 69             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
357+  6939 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
358+  693C 23                   inc     hl
359+  693D 1B                   dec     de
360+  693E 18 F1                jr      .firmware_stream_loop
361+  6940
362+  6940              .firmware_stream_done:
363+  6940                      ; Phase 9: new firmware has jumped to $0000 and sends ACK #A2.
364+  6940 3E 09                ld      a, 9
365+  6942 32 9E 6A             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
366+  6945 CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
367+  6948 DA 52 69             jp      c, .upload_failed
368+  694B FE A2                cp      NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
369+  694D C2 52 69             jp      nz, .upload_failed
370+  6950
371+  6950 AF                   xor     a
372+  6951 C9                   ret
373+  6952
374+  6952              .upload_failed:
375+  6952 37                   scf
376+  6953 C9                   ret
377+  6954
378+  6954              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
379+  6954              ; Standard GS105a ROM transport primitives.
380+  6954              ; Commands use CBIT; data bytes use DBIT. All waits are bounded.
381+  6954              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
382+  6954
383+  6954              ; Probe stock ROM by reading the three-byte RAM size returned by command #20.
384+  6954              gs_boot_probe_rom:
385+  6954 3E 20                ld      a, GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM
386+  6956 CD 82 69             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
387+  6959 D8                   ret     c
388+  695A CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
389+  695D D8                   ret     c
390+  695E 32 94 6A             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_low), a
391+  6961 CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
392+  6964 D8                   ret     c
393+  6965 32 95 6A             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid), a
394+  6968 CD 1A 6A             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
395+  696B D8                   ret     c
396+  696C 32 96 6A             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_high), a
397+  696F 3A 94 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_low)
398+  6972 47                   ld      b, a
399+  6973 3A 95 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid)
400+  6976 B0                   or      b
401+  6977 47                   ld      b, a
402+  6978 3A 96 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_high)
403+  697B B0                   or      b
404+  697C 20 02                jr      nz, .rom_probe_ok
405+  697E 37                   scf
406+  697F C9                   ret
407+  6980              .rom_probe_ok:
408+  6980 B7                   or      a
409+  6981 C9                   ret
410+  6982
411+  6982              ; Long command wait for #F4 cold restart and ROM self-test.
412+  6982              gs_boot_send_command_long:
413+  6982 32 97 6A             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
414+  6985 3A 97 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
415+  6988 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
416+  698A D5                   push    de
417+  698B 16 10                ld      d, 16
418+  698D              .long_outer:
419+  698D 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
420+  6990              .long_inner:
421+  6990 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
422+  6992 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
423+  6995 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
424+  6997 28 0B                jr      z, .long_done
425+  6999 0B                   dec     bc
426+  699A 78                   ld      a, b
427+  699B B1                   or      c
428+  699C 20 F2                jr      nz, .long_inner
429+  699E 15                   dec     d
430+  699F 20 EC                jr      nz, .long_outer
431+  69A1 D1                   pop     de
432+  69A2 37                   scf
433+  69A3 C9                   ret
434+  69A4              .long_done:
435+  69A4 D1                   pop     de
436+  69A5 B7                   or      a
437+  69A6 C9                   ret
438+  69A7
439+  69A7              gs_boot_send_command:
440+  69A7 32 97 6A             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
441+  69AA 3A 97 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
442+  69AD D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
443+  69AF 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
444+  69B2              .command_wait:
445+  69B2 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
446+  69B4 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
447+  69B7 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
448+  69B9 28 07                jr      z, .command_done
449+  69BB 0B                   dec     bc
450+  69BC 78                   ld      a, b
451+  69BD B1                   or      c
452+  69BE 20 F2                jr      nz, .command_wait
453+  69C0 37                   scf
454+  69C1 C9                   ret
455+  69C2              .command_done:
456+  69C2 B7                   or      a
457+  69C3 C9                   ret
458+  69C4
459+  69C4              ; Preload the first argument byte of GS105a commands #13/#14. Those
460+  69C4              ; handlers read this byte before clearing CBIT; later arguments use DBIT.
461+  69C4              gs_boot_write_command_argument:
462+  69C4 32 98 6A             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
463+  69C7 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
464+  69CA              .argument_wait_free:
465+  69CA DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
466+  69CC 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
467+  69CF CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
468+  69D1 28 07                jr      z, .argument_slot_free
469+  69D3 0B                   dec     bc
470+  69D4 78                   ld      a, b
471+  69D5 B1                   or      c
472+  69D6 20 F2                jr      nz, .argument_wait_free
473+  69D8 37                   scf
474+  69D9 C9                   ret
475+  69DA              .argument_slot_free:
476+  69DA 3A 98 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
477+  69DD D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
478+  69DF B7                   or      a
479+  69E0 C9                   ret
480+  69E1
481+  69E1              gs_boot_send_data_byte:
482+  69E1 32 98 6A             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
483+  69E4 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
484+  69E7              .data_wait_free:
485+  69E7 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
486+  69E9 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
487+  69EC CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
488+  69EE 28 07                jr      z, .data_slot_free
489+  69F0 0B                   dec     bc
490+  69F1 78                   ld      a, b
491+  69F2 B1                   or      c
492+  69F3 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_free
493+  69F5 37                   scf
494+  69F6 C9                   ret
495+  69F7              .data_slot_free:
496+  69F7 3A 98 6A             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
497+  69FA D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
498+  69FC 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
499+  69FF              .data_wait_consumed:
500+  69FF DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
501+  6A01 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
502+  6A04 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
503+  6A06 28 07                jr      z, .data_consumed
504+  6A08 0B                   dec     bc
505+  6A09 78                   ld      a, b
506+  6A0A B1                   or      c
507+  6A0B 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_consumed
508+  6A0D 37                   scf
509+  6A0E C9                   ret
510+  6A0F              .data_consumed:
511+  6A0F E5                   push    hl
512+  6A10 2A 9A 6A             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
513+  6A13 23                   inc     hl
514+  6A14 22 9A 6A             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), hl
515+  6A17 E1                   pop     hl
516+  6A18 B7                   or      a
517+  6A19 C9                   ret
518+  6A1A
519+  6A1A              gs_boot_receive_data_byte:
520+  6A1A 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
521+  6A1D              .receive_wait:
522+  6A1D DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
523+  6A1F 32 9D 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
524+  6A22 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
525+  6A24 20 07                jr      nz, .receive_ready
526+  6A26 0B                   dec     bc
527+  6A27 78                   ld      a, b
528+  6A28 B1                   or      c
529+  6A29 20 F2                jr      nz, .receive_wait
530+  6A2B 37                   scf
531+  6A2C C9                   ret
532+  6A2D              .receive_ready:
533+  6A2D DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
534+  6A2F 32 99 6A             ld      (neo_fw_last_rx_byte), a
535+  6A32 B7                   or      a
536+  6A33 C9                   ret
537+  6A34
538+  6A34              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
539+  6A34              ; Project 124 private-protocol waits (custom firmware already running).
540+  6A34              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
541+  6A34              neogs_wait_command_done:
541+  6A34
542+  6A34 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
543+  6A37              .cmd_wait:
544+  6A37 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
545+  6A39 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
546+  6A3B 28 07                jr      z, .cmd_done
547+  6A3D 0B                   dec     bc
548+  6A3E 78                   ld      a, b
549+  6A3F B1                   or      c
550+  6A40 20 F5                jr      nz, .cmd_wait
551+  6A42 37                   scf
552+  6A43 C9                   ret
553+  6A44              .cmd_done:
554+  6A44 B7                   or      a
555+  6A45 C9                   ret
556+  6A46
557+  6A46              neogs_wait_command_done_long:
557+  6A46
558+  6A46 D5                   push    de
559+  6A47 16 10                ld      d, #10
560+  6A49              .cmd_long_outer:
561+  6A49 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
562+  6A4C              .cmd_long_inner:
563+  6A4C DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
564+  6A4E CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
565+  6A50 28 0B                jr      z, .cmd_long_done
566+  6A52 0B                   dec     bc
567+  6A53 78                   ld      a, b
568+  6A54 B1                   or      c
569+  6A55 20 F5                jr      nz, .cmd_long_inner
570+  6A57 15                   dec     d
571+  6A58 20 EF                jr      nz, .cmd_long_outer
572+  6A5A D1                   pop     de
573+  6A5B 37                   scf
574+  6A5C C9                   ret
575+  6A5D              .cmd_long_done:
576+  6A5D D1                   pop     de
577+  6A5E B7                   or      a
578+  6A5F C9                   ret
579+  6A60
580+  6A60              neogs_wait_data_byte:
580+  6A60
581+  6A60 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_BYTE_TIMEOUT
582+  6A63              .data_wait:
583+  6A63 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
584+  6A65 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
585+  6A67 20 07                jr      nz, .data_ready
586+  6A69 0B                   dec     bc
587+  6A6A 78                   ld      a, b
588+  6A6B B1                   or      c
589+  6A6C 20 F5                jr      nz, .data_wait
590+  6A6E 37                   scf
591+  6A6F C9                   ret
592+  6A70              .data_ready:
593+  6A70 DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
594+  6A72 B7                   or      a
595+  6A73 C9                   ret
596+  6A74
597+  6A74              ; TR-DOS NeoGS package loader state and fixed 8-character filename.
598+  6A74 6E 65 6F 70  neo_fw_filename:        db "neoprog "

# file opened: x86_loader.asm
  1+  6A9F              ; =========================================================================
  2+  6A9F              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS task loader + NeoGS DMA / IPC
  3+  6A9F              ; Hardware protocol is based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and
  4+  6A9F              ; ports.inc v0.3. The application-level command IDs/frame are Project 124.
  5+  6A9F              ;
  6+  6A9F              ; DMA mapping used here:
  7+  6A9F              ;   NeoGS physical DMA destination = $00D000
  8+  6A9F              ;   NeoGS Z80 logical DMA frame address = $5000
  9+  6A9F              ;   two-byte length header at $5000; x86 execution begins at $5002.
 10+  6A9F              ; This follows the guide's default mapping: logical $4000-$7FFF is the
 11+  6A9F              ; second half of physical 32K page 1 ($00C000-$00FFFF).
 12+  6A9F              ;
 13+  6A9F              ; Host DMA writes to Spectrum address $0000-$3FFF. For DMA WRITE there is
 14+  6A9F              ; NO dummy write. The dummy access described by the user is only for DMA READ.
 15+  6A9F              ; =========================================================================
 16+  6A9F
 17+  6A9F              ; neogs_io.inc is included once by neogs_driver.asm before this module.
 18+  6A9F
 19+  6A9F              DMA_HOST_WINDOW    EQU #0000
 20+  6A9F              DMA_WINDOW_LIMIT   EQU #3000
 21+  6A9F
 22+  6A9F              EXECUTE_X86_FILE_STAGE:
 22+  6A9F
 23+  6A9F 22 46 77             ld      (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME), hl
 24+  6AA2 32 48 77             ld      (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE), a
 25+  6AA5 ED 73 3E 77          ld      (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP), sp
 26+  6AA9 31 FF BF             ld      sp, #BFFF              ; buffer starts at #C000; stack below it
 27+  6AAC
 28+  6AAC                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_dbg_stack_isolated
 28+  6AAC             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
 28+  6AAC E5          >                    PUSH    HL
 28+  6AAD 21 58 77    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_dbg_stack_isolated
 28+  6AB0 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
 28+  6AB3 E1          >                    POP     HL
 28+  6AB4             >                ENDIF
 29+  6AB4
 30+  6AB4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
 31+  6AB4                      ; Stage 5.0: deterministic diagnostic before accessing TR-DOS.
 32+  6AB4                      ; B8 AA BB 40 90 F4 = MOV AX,BBAA / INC AX / NOP / HLT.
 33+  6AB4                      ; Expected result: status=0, AX=BBAB.
 34+  6AB4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
 35+  6AB4                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_start
 35+  6AB4             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
 35+  6AB4 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
 35+  6AB5 21 8E 77    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_start
 35+  6AB8 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
 35+  6ABB E1          >                    POP     HL
 35+  6ABC             >                ENDIF
 36+  6ABC
 37+  6ABC                      ; Exercise unknown-command release and explicit firmware reset before DMA.
 38+  6ABC 3E FE                ld      a, #FE
 39+  6ABE D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
 40+  6AC0 CD 34 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
 41+  6AC3 DA CC 6D             jp      c, .diag_protocol_error
 42+  6AC6
 43+  6AC6 3E 01                ld      a, CMD_RESET
 44+  6AC8 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
 45+  6ACA CD 34 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
 46+  6ACD DA CC 6D             jp      c, .diag_protocol_error
 47+  6AD0
 48+  6AD0 21 04 77             ld      hl, x86_diag_program
 49+  6AD3 01 06 00             ld      bc, x86_diag_program_end - x86_diag_program
 50+  6AD6 CD F0 6F             call    neogs_send_dma_block
 51+  6AD9 DA C0 6D             jp      c, .diag_dma_error
 52+  6ADC
 53+  6ADC CD 4C 70             call    neogs_run_and_get_result
 54+  6ADF DA CC 6D             jp      c, .diag_protocol_error
 55+  6AE2
 56+  6AE2 3A 51 77             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
 57+  6AE5 B7                   or      a
 58+  6AE6 C2 D8 6D             jp      nz, .diag_execution_error
 59+  6AE9 2A 52 77             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
 60+  6AEC 11 AB BB             ld      de, #BBAB
 61+  6AEF B7                   or      a
 62+  6AF0 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
 63+  6AF2 C2 55 6F             jp      nz, .diag_wrong_result
 64+  6AF5
 65+  6AF5                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_pass
 65+  6AF5             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
 65+  6AF5 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
 65+  6AF6 21 CA 77    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_pass

207+  6C72                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
208+  6C72                      ; Stage 5A: search TR-DOS directory entry.
209+  6C72                      ; Name #5CDD..#5CE4, type #5CE5, per the working project path.
210+  6C72                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
211+  6C72 2A 46 77             ld      hl, (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME)
212+  6C75 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
213+  6C78 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
214+  6C7B ED B0                ldir
215+  6C7D 3A 48 77             ld      a, (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE)
216+  6C80 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
217+  6C83 3E 09                ld      a, 9
218+  6C85 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
219+  6C88 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
220+  6C8A CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
221+  6C8D 79                   ld      a, c
222+  6C8E FE FF                cp      #FF
223+  6C90 CA 76 6F             jp      z, .file_not_found
224+  6C93
225+  6C93                      ; C=#0A returns only the catalogue entry number. Fetch the full
226+  6C93                      ; 16-byte descriptor explicitly with C=#08 before using #5CE8..#5CEC.
227+  6C93 79                   ld      a, c
228+  6C94 F5                   push    af
229+  6C95 AF                   xor     a
230+  6C96 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
231+  6C99 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
232+  6C9C F1                   pop     af
233+  6C9D 0E 08                ld      c, 8
234+  6C9F CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
235+  6CA2 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
236+  6CA5 47                   ld      b, a
237+  6CA6 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
238+  6CA9 B0                   or      b
239+  6CAA C2 8E 6F             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
240+  6CAD
241+  6CAD                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_file_ok
241+  6CAD             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
241+  6CAD E5          >                    PUSH    HL
241+  6CAE 21 7C 79    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_file_ok
241+  6CB1 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
241+  6CB4 E1          >                    POP     HL
241+  6CB5             >                ENDIF
242+  6CB5
243+  6CB5                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
244+  6CB5                      ; Stage 5B: validate descriptor BEFORE reading any sector.
245+  6CB5                      ; #5CE8 = byte length, #5CEA = sector count,
246+  6CB5                      ; #5CEB = first internal sector (0..15), #5CEC = track.
247+  6CB5                      ; The DMA frame window is $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB). Its first two
248+  6CB5                      ; bytes are the length header, so executable payload max is $2FFE.
249+  6CB5                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
250+  6CB5 ED 4B E8 5C          ld      bc, (#5CE8)
251+  6CB9 78                   ld      a, b
252+  6CBA B1                   or      c
253+  6CBB CA 82 6F             jp      z, .empty_file_error
254+  6CBE ED 43 49 77          ld      (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH), bc
255+  6CC2
256+  6CC2                      ; Reject files larger than the executable 12 KB window minus header.
257+  6CC2 78                   ld      a, b
258+  6CC3 FE 2F                cp      #2F
259+  6CC5 38 09                jr      c, .file_length_ok
260+  6CC7 C2 BB 6F             jp      nz, .task_too_large
261+  6CCA 79                   ld      a, c
262+  6CCB FE FF                cp      #FF
263+  6CCD D2 BB 6F             jp      nc, .task_too_large
264+  6CD0              .file_length_ok:
265+  6CD0                      ; Sector count must equal ceil(file_length / 256).
266+  6CD0 2A 49 77             ld      hl, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
267+  6CD3 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
268+  6CD6 19                   add     hl, de
269+  6CD7 5C                   ld      e, h
270+  6CD8 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
271+  6CDB B7                   or      a
272+  6CDC CA 82 6F             jp      z, .empty_file_error
273+  6CDF BB                   cp      e
274+  6CE0 C2 8E 6F             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
275+  6CE3 32 4D 77             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
276+  6CE6
277+  6CE6 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
278+  6CE9 FE 10                cp      16
279+  6CEB D2 8E 6F             jp      nc, .bad_file_descriptor
280+  6CEE 32 4F 77             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
281+  6CF1 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
282+  6CF4 32 4E 77             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
283+  6CF7 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
284+  6CFA 22 4B 77             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
285+  6CFD
286+  6CFD              .read_sector_loop:
287+  6CFD                      ; Read exactly one sector per TR-DOS #05 call.
288+  6CFD 3A 4E 77             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
289+  6D00 57                   ld      d, a
290+  6D01 3A 4F 77             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
291+  6D04 5F                   ld      e, a
292+  6D05 2A 4B 77             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
293+  6D08 06 01                ld      b, 1
294+  6D0A 0E 05                ld      c, 5
295+  6D0C AF                   xor     a
296+  6D0D 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
297+  6D10 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
298+  6D13 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
299+  6D16                      ; C=#05 stores its two-byte error result at #5D0F/#5D10.
300+  6D16 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
301+  6D19 47                   ld      b, a
302+  6D1A 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
303+  6D1D B0                   or      b
304+  6D1E C2 9A 6F             jp      nz, .sector_read_error
305+  6D21
306+  6D21                      ; Advance host buffer by 256 bytes.
307+  6D21 2A 4B 77             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
308+  6D24 11 00 01             ld      de, 256
309+  6D27 19                   add     hl, de
310+  6D28 22 4B 77             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
311+  6D2B
312+  6D2B                      ; TR-DOS sectors are numbered 0..15 in this project convention.
313+  6D2B 3A 4F 77             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
314+  6D2E 3C                   inc     a
315+  6D2F FE 10                cp      16
316+  6D31 38 0D                jr      c, .sector_same_track
317+  6D33 AF                   xor     a
318+  6D34 32 4F 77             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
319+  6D37 3A 4E 77             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
320+  6D3A 3C                   inc     a
321+  6D3B 32 4E 77             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
322+  6D3E 18 03                jr      .sector_advanced
323+  6D40
324+  6D40              .sector_same_track:
325+  6D40 32 4F 77             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
326+  6D43

538+  6FEB              .loader_restore_stack:

540+  6FEE F9                   ld      sp, hl
541+  6FEF C9                   ret
542+  6FF0
543+  6FF0              ; =========================================================================
544+  6FF0              ; NeoGS transport helpers — exact CBIT/DBIT meanings from Guide v0.1.A
545+  6FF0              ; =========================================================================
546+  6FF0
547+  6FF0              ; IN: HL=source in host RAM, BC=payload byte count (1..#2FFE).
548+  6FF0              ; OUT: Carry clear after NeoGS armed DMA, consumed the host end marker,
549+  6FF0              ;      and disabled DMA. Carry set on timeout.
550+  6FF0              ;
551+  6FF0              ; Protocol:
552+  6FF0              ;   host OUT GSCOM,CMD_LOAD_X86 -> CBIT=1
553+  6FF0              ;   NeoGS configures DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (READY)
554+  6FF0              ;   host LDIR to #0000..#3FFF (no dummy write)
555+  6FF0              ;   host OUT GSCOM,#FF -> CBIT=1 (end marker)
556+  6FF0              ;   NeoGS disables DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (DONE)
557+  6FF0              neogs_send_dma_block:
558+  6FF0 78                   ld      a, b
559+  6FF1 B1                   or      c
560+  6FF2 CA 4A 70             jp      z, .dma_bad_length
561+  6FF5 78                   ld      a, b
562+  6FF6 FE 40                cp      #40
563+  6FF8 38 08                jr      c, .dma_length_ok
564+  6FFA C2 4A 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
565+  6FFD 79                   ld      a, c
566+  6FFE B7                   or      a
567+  6FFF C2 4A 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
568+  7002              .dma_length_ok:
569+  7002 22 40 77             ld      (P124_DMA_SOURCE), hl
570+  7005 ED 43 42 77          ld      (P124_DMA_LENGTH), bc
571+  7009
572+  7009                      ; Validate payload + 2-byte length prefix <= $3000.
573+  7009 C5                   push    bc
574+  700A E1                   pop     hl
575+  700B 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
576+  700E 19                   add     hl, de
577+  700F 7C                   ld      a, h
578+  7010 FE 30                cp      #30
579+  7012 38 08                jr      c, .dma_total_length_ok
580+  7014 C2 4A 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
581+  7017 7D                   ld      a, l
582+  7018 B7                   or      a
583+  7019 C2 4A 70             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
584+  701C              .dma_total_length_ok:
585+  701C 3E 02                ld      a, CMD_LOAD_X86
586+  701E D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
587+  7020 CD 34 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
588+  7023 D8                   ret     c
589+  7024
590+  7024                      ; LDIR is used for BOTH pieces of the frame. This is intentional:
591+  7024                      ; the DMA engine observes host memory-write cycles. The first LDIR
592+  7024                      ; writes the two-byte header to host addresses #0000-#0001; the second
593+  7024                      ; writes the payload to #0002 onward. No direct LD (nn),A writes and
594+  7024                      ; no temporary stack are needed.
595+  7024 F3                   di
596+  7025 2A 42 77             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
597+  7028 22 44 77             ld      (P124_DMA_HEADER), hl
598+  702B 21 44 77             ld      hl, P124_DMA_HEADER
599+  702E 11 00 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW
600+  7031 01 02 00             ld      bc, 2
601+  7034 ED B0                ldir
602+  7036
603+  7036 2A 40 77             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_SOURCE)
604+  7039 11 02 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW + 2
605+  703C ED 4B 42 77          ld      bc, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
606+  7040 ED B0                ldir
607+  7042
608+  7042                      ; End marker is a command-byte write; it is NOT a DMA data byte.
609+  7042 3E FF                ld      a, #FF
610+  7044 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
611+  7046 CD 34 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
612+  7049 C9                   ret
613+  704A
614+  704A              .dma_bad_length:
615+  704A 37                   scf
616+  704B C9                   ret
617+  704C
618+  704C              ; Send RUN command and fetch the four-byte application result frame:
619+  704C              ; A5 signature, status, AX low, AX high.
620+  704C              neogs_run_and_get_result:
621+  704C 3E 03                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86
622+  704E D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
623+  7050 CD 6D 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
624+  7053 D8                   ret     c
625+  7054 C3 EC 70             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
626+  7057
627+  7057              ; Execute the actual TR-DOS task with the CS:IP real-mode core.
628+  7057              neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result:
629+  7057 3E 0B                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
630+  7059 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
631+  705B CD 6D 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
632+  705E D8                   ret     c
633+  705F C3 EC 70             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
634+  7062
635+  7062              ; Stage 3A guest BIOS video test. Its output is drained while the NeoGS
636+  7062              ; command remains active; DBIT bytes are never confused with the result frame.
637+  7062              neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result:
638+  7062 3E 0A                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
639+  7064 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
640+  7066 CD 6D 70             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
641+  7069 D8                   ret     c
642+  706A C3 EC 70             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
643+  706D
644+  706D              ; Wait for the RUN command while servicing the bidirectional data channel.
645+  706D              ; Stream protocol: nonzero=character, 00 00=clear, 00 01 row col=set cursor.
646+  706D              ; Each character is sent to emulator printer port #EF and rendered on screen.
647+  706D              neogs_wait_run_done_with_console:
648+  706D D5                   push    de
649+  706E 16 10                ld      d, #10
650+  7070              .run_outer:
651+  7070 21 FF FF             ld      hl, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
652+  7073              .run_inner:
653+  7073 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
654+  7075 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
655+  7077 28 38                jr      z, .no_stream_byte
656+  7079 DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
657+  707B B7                   or      a
658+  707C 28 0C                jr      z, .stream_control
659+  707E F5                   push    af
660+  707F 01 EF 00             ld      bc, #00EF
661+  7082 ED 79                out     (c), a
662+  7084 F1                   pop     af
663+  7085 CD 76 85             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
664+  7088 18 E9                jr      .run_inner
665+  708A
666+  708A              .stream_control:
667+  708A CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
668+  708D 38 2E                jr      c, .run_timeout
669+  708F B7                   or      a
670+  7090 28 1A                jr      z, .stream_clear
671+  7092 FE 01                cp      1
672+  7094 20 DD                jr      nz, .run_inner
673+  7096 CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
674+  7099 38 22                jr      c, .run_timeout
675+  709B 32 57 77             ld      (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW), a
676+  709E CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
677+  70A1 38 1A                jr      c, .run_timeout
678+  70A3 4F                   ld      c, a
679+  70A4 3A 57 77             ld      a, (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW)
680+  70A7 CD 68 85             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_SetCursor
681+  70AA 18 C7                jr      .run_inner
682+  70AC
683+  70AC              .stream_clear:
684+  70AC CD 1D 85             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
685+  70AF 18 C2                jr      .run_inner
686+  70B1
687+  70B1              .no_stream_byte:
688+  70B1 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
689+  70B3 28 0B                jr      z, .run_done
690+  70B5 2B                   dec     hl
691+  70B6 7C                   ld      a, h
692+  70B7 B5                   or      l
693+  70B8 20 B9                jr      nz, .run_inner
694+  70BA 15                   dec     d
695+  70BB 20 B3                jr      nz, .run_outer
696+  70BD              .run_timeout:
697+  70BD D1                   pop     de
698+  70BE 37                   scf
699+  70BF C9                   ret
700+  70C0              .run_done:
701+  70C0 D1                   pop     de
702+  70C1 B7                   or      a
703+  70C2 C9                   ret
704+  70C3
705+  70C3              ; Run the BIOS low-memory POST and fetch the existing four-byte result frame.
706+  70C3              neogs_bios_post_and_get_result:
707+  70C3 3E 07                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_POST
708+  70C5 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
709+  70C7 CD 46 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
710+  70CA D8                   ret     c
711+  70CB C3 EC 70             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
712+  70CE
713+  70CE              ; Run the 8086 interrupt entry/return architectural self-test.
714+  70CE              neogs_int_test_and_get_result:
715+  70CE 3E 08                ld      a, CMD_INT_TEST
716+  70D0 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
717+  70D2 CD 46 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
718+  70D5 D8                   ret     c
719+  70D6 C3 EC 70             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
720+  70D9
721+  70D9              ; Run a real guest INT/IRET handler test and fetch the result frame.
722+  70D9              neogs_int_guest_test_and_get_result:
723+  70D9 3E 09                ld      a, CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
724+  70DB D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
725+  70DD CD 46 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
726+  70E0 D8                   ret     c
727+  70E1 C3 EC 70             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
728+  70E4
729+  70E4              ; Run the NeoGS physical-memory self-test and fetch its result frame.
730+  70E4              neogs_mem_test_and_get_result:
731+  70E4 3E 05                ld      a, CMD_MEM_TEST
732+  70E6 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
733+  70E8 CD 46 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
734+  70EB D8                   ret     c
735+  70EC
736+  70EC              neogs_get_result_frame:
737+  70EC 3E 04                ld      a, CMD_GET_STATUS
738+  70EE D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
739+  70F0
740+  70F0 CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
741+  70F3 D8                   ret     c
742+  70F4 32 50 77             ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC), a
743+  70F7
744+  70F7 CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
745+  70FA D8                   ret     c
746+  70FB 32 51 77             ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS), a
747+  70FE
748+  70FE CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
749+  7101 D8                   ret     c
750+  7102 5F                   ld      e, a
751+  7103
752+  7103 CD 60 6A             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
753+  7106 D8                   ret     c
754+  7107 57                   ld      d, a
755+  7108 ED 53 52 77          ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_AX), de
756+  710C ED 53 1C 64          ld      (X86_RESULT_AX), de
757+  7110
758+  7110 CD 34 6A             call    neogs_wait_command_done
759+  7113 D8                   ret     c
760+  7114
761+  7114 3A 50 77             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC)
762+  7117 FE A5                cp      NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE
763+  7119 28 02                jr      z, .result_frame_ok
764+  711B 37                   scf
765+  711C C9                   ret
766+  711D              .result_frame_ok:
767+  711D B7                   or      a
768+  711E C9                   ret

  1+  8500              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8500              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS 6x8 debug console backend
  3+  8500              ; Lower Spectrum window: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6 pixels x 8 scanlines.
  4+  8500              ; Uses font68.bin (96 glyphs, 8 bytes per glyph). This renderer is separate
  5+  8500              ; from Direct_PrintChar so existing 32-column Commander panels are unchanged.
  6+  8500              ; =========================================================================

 18+  8508 FE 0C                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS

  1+  8767              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8767              ; FILE: zx_main/file_utils.asm
  3+  8767              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS directory utilities
  4+  8767              ; Sector API verified for TR-DOS 5.04T: C=#05, D=track, E=internal sector
  5+  8767              ; 0..15, B=count, HL=destination; error status at #5D0F.
  6+  8767              ; =========================================================================
  7+  8767
  8+  8767              ; IN: CursorY = visible row (2..10)
  9+  8767              ; OUT: HL = 16-byte descriptor in TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER, carry clear.
 10+  8767              ;      HL=0 and carry set for an invalid/empty row.
 11+  8767              GetSelectedFileDescriptor:
 11+  8767
 12+  8767 3A 2E 7D             ld      a, (CursorY)
 13+  876A FE 02                cp      2
 14+  876C 38 1C                jr      c, .bad_row
 15+  876E FE 0B                cp      11
 16+  8770 30 18                jr      nc, .bad_row
 17+  8772 D6 02                sub     2
 18+  8774 6F                   ld      l, a
 19+  8775 26 00                ld      h, 0
 20+  8777 29                   add     hl, hl
 21+  8778 29                   add     hl, hl
 22+  8779 29                   add     hl, hl
 23+  877A 29                   add     hl, hl
 24+  877B 11 00 C0             ld      de, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
 25+  877E 19                   add     hl, de
 26+  877F
 27+  877F                      ; Reject an empty/deleted/invalid catalogue slot.
 28+  877F 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 29+  8780 FE 20                cp      32
 30+  8782 38 06                jr      c, .bad_row
 31+  8784 FE 7F                cp      127
 32+  8786 30 02                jr      nc, .bad_row
 33+  8788 B7                   or      a
 34+  8789 C9                   ret
 35+  878A              .bad_row:

 37+  878D 37                   scf

201+  88D3 D2 F5 88             jp      nc, .directory_done
202+  88D6
203+  88D6                      ; Advance to next 16-byte descriptor. Restore saved slot counter.
204+  88D6 E1                   pop     hl
205+  88D7 11 10 00             ld      de, 16
206+  88DA 19                   add     hl, de
207+  88DB C1                   pop     bc
208+  88DC 05                   dec     b
209+  88DD C2 0E 88             jp      nz, .loop_slots
210+  88E0 C3 F7 88             jp      .directory_success
211+  88E3
212+  88E3              .invalid_slot:
213+  88E3                      ; Drop the per-slot saves, advance HL, and decrement the ORIGINAL
214+  88E3                      ; 128-entry counter. Do not load 16 into BC (that destroys B), and
215+  88E3                      ; do not push the per-slot saves again before re-entering the loop.
216+  88E3 E1                   pop     hl
217+  88E4 C1                   pop     bc
218+  88E5 11 10 00             ld      de, 16
219+  88E8 19                   add     hl, de
220+  88E9 05                   dec     b
221+  88EA C2 0E 88             jp      nz, .loop_slots
222+  88ED C3 F7 88             jp      .directory_success
223+  88F0
224+  88F0              .empty_slot:
225+  88F0                      ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
226+  88F0 E1                   pop     hl
227+  88F1 C1                   pop     bc
228+  88F2 C3 F7 88             jp      .directory_success
229+  88F5
230+  88F5              .directory_done:
231+  88F5                      ; Per-slot BC/HL saves are still present.
232+  88F5 E1                   pop     hl
233+  88F6 C1                   pop     bc

327+  896E                      ; Fill 320 attribute bytes with #4F. The original byte sequence was
328+  896E                      ; LD HL,#5841, leaving DE undefined. This is the correct fill idiom.
329+  896E 21 40 58             ld      hl, #5840
330+  8971 36 4F                ld      (hl), %01001111
